\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
Module for Fourier transform operations in DFT calculations. 

This module contains all variables and procedures related to Fourier transforms in the DFT code. It handles Hartree potential calculations, density transforms, gradient computations, and parallel FFT operations. Supports both periodic and non-\/periodic boundary conditions. 

\label{doc-func-members}

\Hypertarget{namespacefourier__module_a2b658025409528fe988abe7e46c63bc5}\index{fourier\_module@{fourier\_module}!evalgrad@{evalgrad}}
\index{evalgrad@{evalgrad}!fourier\_module@{fourier\_module}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{evalgrad()}{evalgrad()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefourier__module_a2b658025409528fe988abe7e46c63bc5} 
subroutine fourier\+\_\+module\+::evalgrad}



Computes gradient of electron density in Fourier space. 

This subroutine computes the gradient of the electron density using FFT operations. It is designed for GGA exchange-\/correlation functionals but is currently unused in the present version of the code. It performs FFT on density, multiplies by wave vector components, and transforms back to real space. 

Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{fourier__mod_8f90_source_l01484}{1484}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{fourier__mod_8f90_source}{fourier\+\_\+mod.\+f90}}.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{evalvhartreeq()}{evalvhartreeq()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefourier__module_ad7ba13ff09c0afa24583fdf174a6e3b6} 
subroutine fourier\+\_\+module\+::evalvhartreeq}



Evaluates Hartree potential in Fourier space (density-\/independent part) 

This subroutine computes the density-\/independent part of the Hartree potential in Fourier space. It handles both periodic (Ewald) and non-\/periodic boundary conditions, includes dielectric corrections, and performs parallel FFT operations. 

Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{fourier__mod_8f90_source_l00263}{263}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{fourier__mod_8f90_source}{fourier\+\_\+mod.\+f90}}.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{initialize\_fourier()}{initialize\_fourier()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefourier__module_a7c3b80b25797920805fa9ad420d536a0} 
subroutine fourier\+\_\+module\+::initialize\+\_\+fourier}



Initializes Fourier transform parameters and arrays. 

This subroutine initializes all parameters needed for Fourier transform operations including grid dimensions, FFT array sizes, and parallel distribution parameters. It handles double mesh calculations and different scaling factors. 

Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{fourier__mod_8f90_source_l00185}{185}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{fourier__mod_8f90_source}{fourier\+\_\+mod.\+f90}}.

\Hypertarget{namespacefourier__module_a24fe0075a71c877bdebbf93bd81c4c68}\index{fourier\_module@{fourier\_module}!update\_vhartree@{update\_vhartree}}
\index{update\_vhartree@{update\_vhartree}!fourier\_module@{fourier\_module}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{update\_vhartree()}{update\_vhartree()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefourier__module_a24fe0075a71c877bdebbf93bd81c4c68} 
subroutine fourier\+\_\+module\+::update\+\_\+vhartree}



Updates Hartree potential in real space from density-\/dependent part. 

This subroutine computes the density-\/dependent part of the Hartree potential by performing FFT operations on the electron density. It transforms density to Fourier space, multiplies by the precomputed Hartree kernel, and transforms back to real space. Supports parallel execution and includes various corrections. 

Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{fourier__mod_8f90_source_l00638}{638}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{fourier__mod_8f90_source}{fourier\+\_\+mod.\+f90}}.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{upload\_fp()}{upload\_fp()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefourier__module_a01e7d85a58c6c476720d6e2c6370ebe5} 
subroutine fourier\+\_\+module\+::upload\+\_\+fp (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer}]{indmesh}{, }\item[{integer}]{nxi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nxf}{, }\item[{integer}]{nyi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nyf}{, }\item[{integer}]{nzi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nzf}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{ax}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{ay}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8}]{az}{, }\item[{logical}]{add}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Uploads density to Fourier space for FFT calculations. 

This subroutine uploads the electron density from real space to Fourier space for FFT calculations. It handles parallel distribution of grid points across processors and supports both periodic and non-\/periodic boundary conditions. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em indmesh} & Current mesh index \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nxi,nxf} & Starting and ending x grid indices \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nyi,nyf} & Starting and ending y grid indices \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nzi,nzf} & Starting and ending z grid indices \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em ax,ay,az} & Grid spacing in x,y,z directions \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em add} & Flag to add or subtract contributions \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\Hypertarget{namespacefourier__module_a343aeb76576699a734d203efcc70e73f}\index{fourier\_module@{fourier\_module}!upload\_fp\_fromdent@{upload\_fp\_fromdent}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{upload\_fp\_fromdent()}{upload\_fp\_fromdent()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefourier__module_a343aeb76576699a734d203efcc70e73f} 
subroutine fourier\+\_\+module\+::upload\+\_\+fp\+\_\+fromdent}



Uploads electron density from real space to Fourier space. 

This subroutine transfers electron density data from real space to Fourier space for FFT calculations. It handles both regular and Hartree potential calculations, using buffer arrays for parallel distribution of data across processors. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em dent} & Input electron density array \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em do\+\_\+hartree} & Flag for Hartree potential calculation \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em buffer\+\_\+fft} & 2D buffer array for FFT data distribution \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em buffer\+\_\+local} & 1D local buffer for processor-\/specific data \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefourier__module_a8adc6579ae2629c8d7540b5afb93a540} 
subroutine fourier\+\_\+module\+::upload\+\_\+vector\+\_\+global (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{vector}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}8, dimension(nxf -\/ nxi + 1, nyf -\/ nyi + 1, nzf -\/ nzi + 1)}]{vector\+\_\+global}{, }\item[{integer}]{nxi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nxf}{, }\item[{integer}]{nyi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nyf}{, }\item[{integer}]{nzi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nzf}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Uploads local vector to global array for parallel processing. 

This subroutine distributes a local vector across processors and assembles it into a global array. It handles periodic boundary conditions and supports different grid sizes for parallel FFT operations. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em vector} & Local vector to be uploaded \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em vector\+\_\+global} & Global array to store the distributed vector \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nxi,nxf} & Starting and ending x grid indices \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nyi,nyf} & Starting and ending y grid indices \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nzi,nzf} & Starting and ending z grid indices \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{upload\_vhartree()}{upload\_vhartree()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefourier__module_a38b8ed98eebb69707e9501c0da649676} 
subroutine fourier\+\_\+module\+::upload\+\_\+vhartree (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer}]{indmesh}{, }\item[{integer}]{scalea}{, }\item[{integer}]{nxi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nxf}{, }\item[{integer}]{nyi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nyf}{, }\item[{integer}]{nzi}{, }\item[{integer}]{nzf}{, }\item[{logical}]{add}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Uploads Hartree potential to fine mesh for double mesh calculations. 

This subroutine interpolates the Hartree potential from the coarse mesh to a fine mesh around atomic positions. It uses linear or cubic interpolation and supports parallel execution for double mesh calculations. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em indmesh} & Current mesh index \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em scalea} & Scaling factor for mesh refinement \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nxi,nxf} & Starting and ending x grid indices \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nyi,nyf} & Starting and ending y grid indices \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nzi,nzf} & Starting and ending z grid indices \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em add} & Flag to add or subtract contributions \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{upload\_vhartree\_fromfp()}{upload\_vhartree\_fromfp()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefourier__module_aa92b7a156471e4501ecf704985068e18} 
subroutine fourier\+\_\+module\+::upload\+\_\+vhartree\+\_\+fromfp}



Uploads Hartree potential from Fourier space to real space. 

This subroutine transfers the Hartree potential from Fourier space representation back to real space grid points. It handles parallel distribution of data across processors and supports both periodic and non-\/periodic boundary conditions. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Uses MPI gather operations for parallel execution and includes special handling for periodic boundary conditions through iespbc flag 
\end{DoxyNote}


Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{fourier__mod_8f90_source_l01937}{1937}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{fourier__mod_8f90_source}{fourier\+\_\+mod.\+f90}}.
